Required columns (exact names may vary slightly in inputs; resolve by case-insensitive match).\n    rid_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Return ID\", \"ReturnId\", \"Return\"])\n    lid_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Line ID\", \"LineId\", \"Line\"])\n    status_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Event Status\", \"Status\", \"EventStatus\"])\n    disp_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Final Disposition\", \"Disposition\", \"FinalDisposition\"])\n\n    if rid_col is None or lid_col is None or status_col is None or disp_col is None:\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"Event log missing required columns. \"\n            f\"Found columns: {list(events_df.columns)}\"\n        )\n\n    time_col = find_col(\n        events_df,\n        [\n            \"Event Time\",\n            \"Event Timestamp\",\n            \"Timestamp\",\n            \"Event Date\",\n            \"Event Datetime\",\n            \"Event Time UTC\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n    # Filter to COMPLETED (case-insensitive compare of values)\n    status_upper = events_df[status_col].astype(str).str.strip().str.upper()\n    completed = events_df.loc[status_upper == \"COMPLETED\"].copy()\n\n    if completed.empty:\n        # Return empty with required key columns so downstream merge works.\n        return completed[[rid_col, lid_col, disp_col]].copy()\n\n    completed[\"__rownum\"] = range(len(completed))\n\n    if time_col is None:\n        completed[\"__event_ts\"] = pd.NaT\n    else:\n        completed[\"__event_ts\"] = pd.to_datetime(completed[time_col], errors=\"coerce\", utc=False)\n\n    # 1a. Load data\n    plan_df = pd.read_excel(PLAN_XLSX)\n    events_df = pd.read_excel(EVENTS_XLSX)\n    alias_df = pd.read_excel(ALIAS_XLSX)\n\n    print(\"Loaded inputs:\")\n    print(f\"  plan_df:   {plan_df.shape} columns={list(plan_df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"  events_df: {events_df.shape} columns={list(events_df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"  alias_df:  {alias_df.shape} columns={list(alias_df.columns)}\")\n\n    # 1b. Build alias lookup for normalization\n    alias_dict = build_alias_dict(alias_df)\n\n    # Merge kept event onto plan rows\n    merged = formatted_base.merge(\n        kept_events,\n        on=[\"Return ID\", \"Line ID\"],\n        how=\"left\",\n        validate=\"m:1\",\n    )\n\n    has_event = merged[\"Final Disposition\"].notna()\n\n    merged[\"Missing Final Event\"] = (~has_event).astype(int)\n\n    merged[\"__final_norm\"] = merged[\"Final Disposition\"].apply(lambda v: normalize_disposition(v, alias_dict))\n\n    planned_norm = merged[\"Planned Disposition\"].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()\n    final_norm = (\n        merged[\"__final_norm\"].where(has_event, \"\")\n        .fillna(\"\")\n        .astype(str)\n        .str.strip()\n        .str.lower()\n    )\n\n    merged[\"Disposition Mismatch\"] = (has_event & (final_norm != planned_norm)).astype(int)\n    merged[\"Total Errors\"] = merged[\"Missing Final Event\"] + merged[\"Disposition Mismatch\"]\n\n    def error_summary_row(missing: int, mismatch: int) -> str:\n        if missing == 0 and mismatch == 0:\n            return \"None\"\n        if missing == 1 and mismatch == 0:\n            return \"Missing Final Event\"\n        if missing == 0 and mismatch == 1:\n            return \"Disposition Mismatch\"\n        return \"Missing Final Event, Disposition Mismatch\"\n\n    merged[\"Error Summary\"] = [\n        error_summary_row(int(m), int(mm))\n        for m, mm in zip(merged[\"Missing Final Event\"], merged[\"Disposition Mismatch\"], strict=False)\n    ]\n\n    formatted_df = merged[\n        TARGET_8_HEADERS\n        + [\n            \"Missing Final Event\",\n            \"Disposition Mismatch\",\n            \"Total Errors\",\n            \"Error Summary\",\n        ]\n    ].copy()\n\n    # 1g. Summary worksheet\n    summary_grouped = (\n        formatted_df.groupby([\"Warehouse\", \"Carrier\"], dropna=False)[\n            [\"Missing Final Event\", \"Disposition Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]\n        ]\n        .sum()\n        .reset_index()\n    )\n\n    summary_filtered = summary_grouped.loc[summary_grouped[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n    summary_filtered = summary_filtered.sort_values(by=[\"Warehouse\", \"Carrier\"], ascending=[True, True])\n\n    grand_totals = formatted_df[[\"Missing Final Event\", \"Disposition Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]].sum()\n    grand_total_row = pd.DataFrame(\n        [\n            {\n                \"Warehouse\": \"Grand Total\",\n                \"Carrier\": \"-\",\n                \"Missing Final Event\": int(grand_totals[\"Missing Final Event\"]),\n                \"Disposition Mismatch\": int(grand_totals[\"Disposition Mismatch\"]),\n                \"Total Errors\": int(grand_totals[\"Total Errors\"]),\n            }\n        ]\n    )\n\n    summary_df = pd.concat([summary_filtered, grand_total_row], ignore_index=True)\n    summary_df = summary_df.rename(\n        columns={\n            \"Missing Final Event\": \"Missing Final Events\",\n            \"Disposition Mismatch\": \"Disposition Mismatches\",\n        }\n    )\n\n    # 1h. Word executive summary\n    totals_missing = int(grand_totals[\"Missing Final Event\"])\n    totals_mismatch = int(grand_totals[\"Disposition Mismatch\"])\n    totals_errors = int(grand_totals[\"Total Errors\"])\n\n    top_returns = (\n        formatted_df.groupby(\"Return ID\", dropna=False)[\"Total Errors\"].sum().sort_values(ascending=False)\n    )\n    top_returns = top_returns.loc[top_returns > 0]\n    top_ids = list(top_returns.head(2).index)\n\n    doc = Document()\n    doc.add_heading(\"Returns Disposition Audit — Executive Summary\", level=1)\n\n    # 3–6 sentences, plain language, include totals + actionable recommendation + top Return IDs\n    sentences: list[str] = []\n    sentences.append(\n        \"This audit performs two checks on each planned return line: (1) Missing Final Event, meaning no COMPLETED disposition event was recorded for that Return ID and Line ID, and (2) Disposition Mismatch, meaning the final recorded (and alias-normalized) disposition does not match the planned disposition.\"\n    )\n    sentences.append(\n        f\"Across all planned lines, we found {totals_missing} Missing Final Events and {totals_mismatch} Disposition Mismatches, for a total of {totals_errors} errors/exceptions.\"\n    )\n\n    if len(top_ids) >= 2:\n        sentences.append(\n            f\"The highest-priority Return IDs by exception count include {top_ids[0]} and {top_ids[1]}; these should be reviewed first to confirm whether events are missing, mis-logged, or incorrectly planned.\"\n        )\n    elif len(top_ids) == 1:\n        sentences.append(\n            f\"The highest-priority Return ID by exception count is {top_ids[0]}; it should be reviewed first to confirm whether events are missing, mis-logged, or incorrectly planned.\"\n        )\n    else:\n        sentences.append(\n            \"No Return IDs exhibited exceptions in this dataset; continue monitoring to ensure event logging remains complete and consistent with planning.\"\n        )\n\n    sentences.append(\n        \"Recommended next steps are to investigate the root causes for missing COMPLETED events (e.g., scanner/workflow gaps or integration delays) and to standardize disposition values at the source (or expand alias coverage) to reduce mismatch rates and downstream rework.\"\n    )\n\n    doc.add_paragraph(\" \".join(sentences))\n    doc.save(OUT_DOCX)\n\n    # Validation prints\n    print(f\"\\nWrote: {OUT_XLSX}\")\n    print(f\"Wrote: {OUT_DOCX}\")\n\n    # Print a few sample error rows\n    error_rows = formatted_df.loc[formatted_df[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].head(10)\n    if not error_rows.empty:\n        print(\"\\nSample rows with errors (Formatted Data):\")\n        print(error_rows.to_string(index=False))\n\n    print(\"\\nSummary table:\")\n    print(summary_df.to_string(index=False))\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7fe0bf30-c418-4ad8-a42c-1c4f4b7ec26e","uuid":"3d811e2a-d30e-4b6e-96a2-a8a032511b62"}

Required columns (exact names may vary slightly in inputs; resolve by case-insensitive match).\n    rid_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Return ID\", \"ReturnId\", \"Return\"])\n    lid_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Line ID\", \"LineId\", \"Line\"])\n    status_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Event Status\", \"Status\", \"EventStatus\"])\n    disp_col = find_col(events_df, [\"Final Disposition\", \"Disposition\", \"FinalDisposition\"])\n\n    if rid_col is None or lid_col is None or status_col is None or disp_col is None:\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"Event log missing required columns. \"\n            f\"Found columns: {list(events_df.columns)}\"\n        )\n\n    time_col = find_col(\n        events_df,\n        [\n            \"Event Time\",\n            \"Event Timestamp\",\n            \"Timestamp\",\n            \"Event Date\",\n            \"Event Datetime\",\n            \"Event Time UTC\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n    # Filter to COMPLETED (case-insensitive compare of values)\n    status_upper = events_df[status_col].astype(str).str.strip().str.upper()\n    completed = events_df.loc[status_upper == \"COMPLETED\"].copy()\n\n    if completed.empty:\n        # Return empty with required key columns so downstream merge works.\n        return completed[[rid_col, lid_col, disp_col]].copy()\n\n    completed[\"__rownum\"] = range(len(completed))\n\n    if time_col is None:\n        completed[\"__event_ts\"] = pd.NaT\n    else:\n        completed[\"__event_ts\"] = pd.to_datetime(completed[time_col], errors=\"coerce\", utc=False)\n\n    # Sort so \"latest\" is last per (Return ID, Line ID).\n    # For rows with NaT timestamps, __rownum provides a deterministic tiebreak.\n    completed = completed.sort_values(\n        by=[rid_col, lid_col, \"__event_ts\", \"__rownum\"],\n        ascending=[True, True, True, True],\n        na_position=\"first\",\n    )\n\n    kept = completed.drop_duplicates(subset=[rid_col, lid_col], keep=\"last\").copy()\n\n    # Normalize column names we will merge on\n    kept = kept.rename(columns={rid_col: \"Return ID\", lid_col: \"Line ID\", disp_col: \"Final Disposition\"})\n    kept = kept[[\"Return ID\", \"Line ID\", \"Final Disposition\"]]\n    return kept\n\n\ndef normalize_disposition(value, alias_dict: dict[str, str]) -> str | None:\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return None\n    raw = str(value).strip()\n    if not raw:\n        return \"\"\n    return alias_dict.get(raw.lower(), raw)\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    # Summary worksheet\n    summary_grouped = (\n        formatted_df.groupby([\"Warehouse\", \"Carrier\"], dropna=False)[\n            [\"Missing Final Event\", \"Disposition Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]\n        ]\n        .sum()\n        .reset_index()\n    )\n\n    summary_filtered = summary_grouped.loc[summary_grouped[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n    summary_filtered = summary_filtered.sort_values(by=[\"Warehouse\", \"Carrier\"], ascending=[True, True])\n\n    grand_totals = formatted_df[[\"Missing Final Event\", \"Disposition Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]].sum()\n    grand_total_row = pd.DataFrame(\n        [\n            {\n                \"Warehouse\": \"Grand Total\",\n                \"Carrier\": \"-\",\n                \"Missing Final Event\": int(grand_totals[\"Missing Final Event\"]),\n                \"Disposition Mismatch\": int(grand_totals[\"Disposition Mismatch\"]),\n                \"Total Errors\": int(grand_totals[\"Total Errors\"]),\n            }\n        ]\n    )\n\n    summary_df = pd.concat([summary_filtered, grand_total_row], ignore_index=True)\n    summary_df = summary_df.rename(\n        columns={\n            \"Missing Final Event\": \"Missing Final Events\",\n            \"Disposition Mismatch\": \"Disposition Mismatches\",\n        }\n    )\n\n    # 1h.
